\frac{1}{p!(p^{\prime})!}=\frac{1}{q!}\binom{q}{p}
. Then we replace
\sum_{p=0}^{q}\binom{q}{p}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,p}}\sum_{\nu^{\prime}\in S_{I^{\prime},q-p}}
with
\sum_{J\subset[q]}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,J}}\sum_{\nu^{\prime}\in S_{I^{\prime},[q]\setminus J}}
, where
[q]=\{1,2,\ldots,q\}
as usual and
S_{I,J}
is the set of injections from
I
to
J
, etc. Thus, letting
W=\{1,\dots,r\}
, we find

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{m\alpha}}=\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{q}\frac{1}{q!}\int_{\Omega^{q}}d^{2q}x\prod_{j=1}^{q}\alpha(x_{j})

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{L\subset W}\sum_{I\subset L}\sum_{I^{\prime}\subset W\setminus L}\sum_{J\subset[q]}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,J}}\sum_{\nu^{\prime}\in S_{I^{\prime},[q]\setminus J}}\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\prod_{i^{\prime}\in I^{\prime}}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i^{\prime}}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu^{\prime}(i^{\prime})}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{C\subset J\setminus\nu(I)}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j\in L\setminus I}\varepsilon_{w_{j}}\prod_{k\in C}\varepsilon_{x_{k}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset[q]\setminus(J\cup\nu^{\prime}(I^{\prime}))}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j^{\prime}\in(W\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{w_{j^{\prime}}}\prod_{k^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{x_{k^{\prime}}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{J\setminus(\nu(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus(J\cup\nu^{\prime}(I^{\prime})\cup C^{\prime})}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B^{\prime}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}.

Let us next combine the sum over
\nu,\nu^{\prime}
into a single sum over
\tau\in S_{I\cup I^{\prime},[q]}
, subject to the condition that
\tau(I)\subset J
and
\tau(I^{\prime})\subset[q]\setminus J
. We can rewrite our correlation function as

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{m\alpha}}=\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{q}\frac{1}{q!}\int_{\Omega^{q}}d^{2r}x\prod_{j=1}^{q}\alpha(x_{j})

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{L\subset W}\sum_{I\subset L}\sum_{I^{\prime}\subset W\setminus L}\sum_{\tau\in S_{I\cup I^{\prime},[q]}}\prod_{i\in I\cup I^{\prime}}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\tau(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\sum_{J\subset[q]}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau(I)\subset J,\tau(I^{\prime})\subset[q]\setminus J\}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{C\subset J\setminus\tau(I)}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j\in L\setminus I}\varepsilon_{w_{j}}\prod_{k\in C}\varepsilon_{x_{k}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset[q]\setminus(J\cup\tau(I^{\prime}))}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j^{\prime}\in(W\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{w_{j^{\prime}}}\prod_{k^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{x_{k^{\prime}}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{J\setminus(\tau(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus(J\cup\tau(I^{\prime})\cup C^{\prime})}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B^{\prime}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}.

We will now rewrite the sums over
C,C^{\prime}
as sums over
D=C\cup C^{\prime}\subset[q]\setminus\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})
and
C
. Then we may pull them out of the
J
sum, as long as we enforce
C\subset J
and
D\setminus C\subset[q]\setminus J
later on:

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{m\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{q}\frac{1}{q!}\int_{\Omega^{q}}d^{2q}x\prod_{j=1}^{q}\alpha(x_{j})\sum_{L\subset W}\sum_{I\subset L}\sum_{I^{\prime}\subset W\setminus L}\sum_{\tau\in S_{I\cup I^{\prime},[q]}}\prod_{i\in I\cup I^{\prime}}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\tau(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt\times\sum_{D\subset[q]\setminus\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})}\sum_{C\subset D}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j\in L\setminus I}\varepsilon_{w_{j}}\prod_{k\in C}\varepsilon_{x_{k}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j^{\prime}\in(W\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{w_{j^{\prime}}}\prod_{k^{\prime}\in D\setminus C}\varepsilon_{x_{k^{\prime}}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt\times\sum_{J\subset[q]}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau(I)\subset J,\tau(I^{\prime})\subset[q]\setminus J\}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\{C\subset J,D\setminus C\subset[q]\setminus J\}}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{J\setminus(\tau(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt\times\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus(J\cup\tau(I^{\prime})\cup(D\setminus C))}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B^{\prime}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}.

We will now try to merge
\Lambda,\Lambda^{\prime}
sums into a single sum, since
\Lambda\cup\Lambda^{\prime}
is a partition of
[q]\setminus(\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D)
.
We can thus write

\displaystyle\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{J\setminus(\tau(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus(J\cup\tau(I^{\prime})\cup(D\setminus C))}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B^{\prime}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus(\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|}\prod_{B\in\lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\varepsilon_{B}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{J\setminus(\tau(I)\cup C)}}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus(J\cup\tau(I^{\prime})\cup(D\setminus C))}}|\Lambda|!|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda=\Lambda\cup\Lambda^{\prime}\}}.

We will now evaluate the remaining
J
and
\Lambda,\Lambda^{\prime}
sums. First of all, we note that under the constraints on
J
, we can uniquely write
J=\tau(I)\cup C\cup E
where
E
ranges over subsets of
[q]\setminus(\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D)
. Note that all of the sets are disjoint so we have
J\setminus(\tau(I)\cup C)=E
and
[q]\setminus(J\cup\tau(I^{\prime})\cup(D\setminus C))=([q]\setminus(\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D))\setminus E
.

This means that the
J,\Lambda,\Lambda^{\prime}
sums can be combined into

\displaystyle\sum_{E\subset[q]\setminus(\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D)}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{E}}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{([q]\setminus(\tau(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D))\setminus E}}|\Lambda|!|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda=\Lambda\cup\Lambda^{\prime}\}}.

To evaluate this sum, note that for any finite set
A

\displaystyle\sum_{B\subset A}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{A\setminus B}}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{B}}|\Lambda|!|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda=\Lambda\cup\Lambda^{\prime}\}}=\sum_{\nu:\nu\subset\lambda}|\nu|!(|\lambda|-|\nu|)!.

We can now split this according to the size of
\nu
:

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu:\nu\subset\lambda}|\nu|!(|\lambda|-|\nu|)!=\sum_{s=0}^{|\lambda|}\sum_{\nu\subset\lambda:|\nu|=s}s!(|\lambda|-s)!=\sum_{s=0}^{|\lambda|}s!(|\lambda|-s)!\sum_{\nu\subset\lambda:|\nu|=s}1.

Now, given
\lambda
, it has precisely
\binom{|\lambda|}{s}
subsets of size
s
, so we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\nu:\nu\subset\lambda}|\nu|!(|\lambda|-|\nu|)!=\sum_{s=0}^{|\lambda|}s!(|\lambda|-s)!\frac{|\lambda|!}{s!(|\lambda|-s)!}=(|\lambda|+1)!.

Relabeling
q,\lambda,\tau
as
p,\Lambda,\nu
, we thus have

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}\prod_{l=1}^{r}(\varepsilon_{w_{l}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{l}})\rangle_{m\alpha}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{m\alpha}}=\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-m)^{p}}{p!\cdot\pi^{p}}\int_{\Omega^{p}}d^{2p}x\alpha(x_{1})\cdots\alpha(x_{p})

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{L\subset\{1,\dots,r\}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\subset L,\\
I^{\prime}\subset\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus L\end{subarray}}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I\cup I^{\prime},[p]}}\sum_{D\subset\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus\nu(I\cup I^{\prime})}\sum_{C\sqcup C^{\prime}=D}\prod_{i\in I\cup I^{\prime}}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j\in L\setminus I}\varepsilon_{w_{j}}\prod_{k\in C}\varepsilon_{x_{k}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j^{\prime}\in(\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{w_{j^{\prime}}}\prod_{k^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{x_{k^{\prime}}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus(\nu(I\cup I^{\prime})\cup D)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}(|\Lambda|+1)!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\prod_{l\in B}\varepsilon_{x_{l}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}.

Next we use Lemma
8.4
on the last line, interchange the order of the
L
-sum and the
I,I^{\prime}
-sum and simultaneously decompose the sum over
I,I^{\prime}
according to the sum over
E=I\cup I^{\prime}
. We find

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}\prod_{l=1}^{r}(\varepsilon_{w_{l}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{l}})\rangle_{m\alpha}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{m\alpha}}=\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-m)^{p}}{p!\cdot\pi^{p}}\int_{\Omega^{p}}d^{2p}x\alpha(x_{1})\cdots\alpha(x_{p})

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{E\subset\{1,\dots,r\}}\sum_{I\sqcup I^{\prime}=E}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}L\subset\{1,\dots,r\}:\\
I\subset L,I^{\prime}\subset\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus L\end{subarray}}\sum_{\nu\in S_{E,[p]}}\sum_{D\subset\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus\nu(E)}\sum_{C\sqcup C^{\prime}=D}\prod_{i\in E}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j\in L\setminus I}\varepsilon_{w_{j}}\prod_{k\in C}\varepsilon_{x_{k}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j^{\prime}\in(\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{w_{j^{\prime}}}\prod_{k^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{x_{k^{\prime}}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus(\nu(E)\cup D)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{l\in B}(\varepsilon_{x_{l}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{l}})\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}.

Our next remark is that
L\setminus I\sqcup[(\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}]=\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus E
, and summing over
L
is equivalent to summing over the ways to partition
\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus E
into two parts (
J=L\setminus I
and
J^{\prime}=(\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus L)\setminus I^{\prime}
), so we can write this as

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}\prod_{l=1}^{r}(\varepsilon_{w_{l}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{l}})\rangle_{m\alpha}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{m\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\!\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-m)^{p}}{p!\cdot\pi^{p}}\!\int_{\Omega^{p}}\!d^{2p}x\alpha(x_{1})\cdots\alpha(x_{p})\!\sum_{E\subset\{1,\dots,r\}}\sum_{I\sqcup I^{\prime}=E}\sum_{J\sqcup J^{\prime}=\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus E}\sum_{\nu\in S_{E,[p]}}\sum_{D\subset\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus\nu(E)}\sum_{C\sqcup C^{\prime}=D}

\displaystyle\quad\times\prod_{i\in E}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j\in J}\varepsilon_{w_{j}}\prod_{k\in C}\varepsilon_{x_{k}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\mu_{U}\Psi_{Z}\prod_{j^{\prime}\in J^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{w_{j^{\prime}}}\prod_{k^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}\varepsilon_{x_{k^{\prime}}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\sigma_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus(\nu(E)\cup D)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{l\in B}(\varepsilon_{x_{l}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{l}})\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-m)^{p}}{p!\cdot\pi^{p}}\int_{\Omega^{p}}d^{2p}x\alpha(x_{1})\cdots\alpha(x_{p})\sum_{E\subset\{1,\dots,r\}}\sum_{I\sqcup I^{\prime}=E}\sum_{\nu\in S_{E,p}}\sum_{D\subset\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus\nu(E)}

\displaystyle\quad\times\prod_{i\in E}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}\prod_{j\in\{1,\dots,r\}\setminus E}(\varepsilon_{w_{j}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{j}})\prod_{k\in D}(\varepsilon_{x_{k}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{k}})\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{\{1,\dots,p\}\setminus(\nu(E)\cup D)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{l\in B}(\varepsilon_{x_{l}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{l}})\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}.

It remains to note that nothing in the sum depends on
I,I^{\prime}
, so the sum over
I,I^{\prime}
produces a factor of
2^{|E|}
. To conclude, we note that by independence

2^{|E|}\prod_{i\in E}\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}=\prod_{i\in E}\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}.

Relabeling
E,D
as
I,C
yields the claim.
∎

8.3.
Proof of Theorem
1.4

We can finally prove our main result about the Ising model.

Proof of Theorem
1.4
.

The points (1) and (2), i.e. existence, boundedness, analyticity, and local continuity, follow from our work in Sections
6
and
7
. Namely, pure spin correlations exist due to Proposition
6.44
; spin-weighted correlations of other fields may then be constructed from (
7.2.14
) and (
7.3.1
). Both are analytic, with series expansion under usual conditions, by Proposition
7.2
and Lemma
7.12
. Then, from Remark
7.1
, it is easy to see that local uniform boundedness and continuity hold for
\left\langle\sigma_{U}\mu_{V}\psi_{Z}\psi^{*}_{W}\varepsilon_{A}\right\rangle_{m\alpha;\Omega}
within any single radius of convergence; then, since any radius of convergence is bounded below by
c=c(\Omega,M=2,\kappa_{\infty},\kappa_{b},\delta_{all},|U|+|V|+|Z|+|W|+|A|)
, we obtain the local uniform bound and continuity for
m=1
by adding at most
2/c
copies of the single bound as in the proof of Corollary
6.39
.

Multiplying the series expansions from Lemma
8.5
and Theorem
8.2
, using the moment to cumulant map (
1.4.3
), we have,

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}=\!\sum_{p,q=0}^{\infty}\!\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{p+q}\!\frac{1}{p!q!}\int_{\Omega^{p+q}}\!d^{2p}xd^{2q}y\prod_{j=1}^{p}\alpha(x_{j})\prod_{k=1}^{q}\alpha(y_{k})

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{I\subset W}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,[p]}}\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C\subset[p]\setminus\nu(I)}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W\setminus I}\prod_{s\in C}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{[p]\setminus(\nu(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset[q]}\bigg\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{y_{s^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[q]\setminus C^{\prime}}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{y_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

We then call
p+q=r
and define
x_{p+j}=y_{j}
, which allows us to write this as

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}=\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{r}\frac{1}{r!}\sum_{p=0}^{r}\binom{r}{p}\int_{\Omega^{r}}d^{2r}x\prod_{j=1}^{r}\alpha(x_{j})

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{I\subset W}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,[p]}}\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C\subset[p]\setminus\nu(I)}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W\setminus I}\prod_{s\in C}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{[p]\setminus(\nu(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset(p,r]}\bigg\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{(p,r]\setminus C^{\prime}}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

Due to the symmetry, we find from simply relabeling the integration variables that we can interpret the sum
\sum_{p=0}^{r}\binom{r}{p}
as a sum over all possible subsets
J\subset[r]
, however we must replace e.g.
S_{I,[p]}
with
S_{I,J}
(injections from
I
to
J
) and so on. This lets us write

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}=\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{r}\frac{1}{r!}\int_{\Omega^{r}}d^{2r}x\prod_{j=1}^{r}\alpha(x_{j})\sum_{I\subset W}\sum_{J\subset[r]}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,J}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\sum_{C\subset J\setminus\nu(I)}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W\setminus I}\prod_{s\in C}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{J\setminus(\nu(I)\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset[r]\setminus J}\bigg\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(J\cup C^{\prime})}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

We now write the sum
\sum_{J\subset[r]}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,J}}
in a slightly different way. Note that each pair
(J,\nu)
can be (bijectively) identified with a triple
(K,E,\nu)
, where
\nu:I\to K\subset J
is a bijection (we abuse notation as
\nu\in S_{I,J}
maps to
J
while it only takes values in
K
), and
E=J\setminus K
. Thus we can write

\displaystyle\sum_{J\subset[r]}\sum_{\nu\in S_{I,J}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}K\subset[r]:\\
|K|=|I|\end{subarray}}\sum_{\nu:I\leftrightarrow K}\sum_{E\subset[r]\setminus K}.

With this representation, we have

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}=\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{r}\frac{1}{r!}\int_{\Omega^{r}}d^{2r}x\prod_{j=1}^{r}\alpha(x_{j})\sum_{I\subset W}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}K\subset[r]:\\
|K|=|I|\end{subarray}}\sum_{\nu:I\leftrightarrow K}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\!\!\sum_{E\subset[r]\setminus K}\sum_{C\subset E}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W\setminus I}\prod_{s\in C}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\times\!\!\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{E\setminus C}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\sigma_{U}\widetilde{\sigma}_{U}\rangle_{0}}\!\!\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset([r]\setminus K)\setminus E}\!\!\bigg\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{([r]\setminus K)\setminus(E\cup C^{\prime})}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

We now swap the order of the
E
and
C
sums. This is convenient to do by writing
E=C\cup F
with
F\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C)
. Note that the constraint on
C^{\prime}
now becomes
C^{\prime}\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup F)
so we have

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}=\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{r}\frac{1}{r!}\int_{\Omega^{r}}d^{2r}x\prod_{j=1}^{r}\alpha(x_{j})\sum_{I\subset W}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}K\subset[r]:\\
|K|=|I|\end{subarray}}\sum_{\nu:I\leftrightarrow K}

\displaystyle\quad\times\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)\sum_{C\subset[r]\setminus K}\bigg\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W\setminus I}\prod_{s\in C}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s}}\bigg\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{F\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{F}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup F)}\frac{\left\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s^{\prime}}}\right\rangle_{0}}{{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup C^{\prime}\cup F)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

Let us try to perform this sum. First of all, there are two cases we will consider separately: either
C^{\prime}=\emptyset
or
C^{\prime}\neq\emptyset
.

Case one

In the first case, we end up with the sum

\displaystyle\sum_{F\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{F}}\!(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\!\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\!

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup F)}}\!(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\!\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

We insert within these sums
1=\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda=\Lambda\cup\Lambda^{\prime}\}}
. We then pull out the
\lambda
sum and note given
\lambda
and
F
,
\Lambda
and
\Lambda^{\prime}
are uniquely determined, and summing over
F
is equivalent to just summing over subpartitions (subsets) of
\lambda
. So this sum becomes

\displaystyle\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}\!\!(-1)^{|\lambda|}\!\sum_{\tau:\tau\subset\lambda}\!|\tau|!(|\lambda|-|\tau|)!\prod_{B\in\tau}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\lambda\setminus\tau}\!\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

Before trying to further manipulate this quantity, let us look at the second case.

Case two

In this case, we insert
1=\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda=\Lambda\cup\Lambda^{\prime}\cup\{C^{\prime}\}\}}
and again pull out the
\lambda
sum. The idea is now to identify
\Lambda\cup\{C^{\prime}\}
as a (non-empty) subpartition of
\lambda
. There is a minor difference to earlier cases in that given a subpartition
\tau\subset\lambda
, there are
|\tau|
different ways we can choose the block
C^{\prime}
(
\Lambda=\tau\setminus\{C^{\prime}\}
and
\Lambda^{\prime}=\lambda\setminus\tau
are then fixed). To be more precise, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{F\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{F}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{C^{\prime}\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup F)}\frac{\left\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s^{\prime}}}\right\rangle_{0}}{{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup C^{\prime}\cup F)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|-1}\sum_{\tau:\tau\subset\lambda}\sum_{F\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{F}}(-1)^{|\Lambda|}|\Lambda|!\prod_{B\in\Lambda}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{\emptyset\neq C^{\prime}\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup F)}\frac{\left\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{s^{\prime}\in C^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s^{\prime}}}\right\rangle_{0}}{{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup C^{\prime}\cup F)}}(-1)^{|\Lambda^{\prime}|}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau=\Lambda\cup C^{\prime},\lambda\setminus\tau=\Lambda^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|-1}\sum_{\tau:\emptyset\neq\tau\subset\lambda}\prod_{B\in\tau}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\lambda\setminus\tau}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{F\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}\sum_{\Lambda\in\Pi_{F}}|\Lambda|!\sum_{\emptyset\neq C^{\prime}\subset[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup F)}\sum_{\Lambda^{\prime}\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C\cup C^{\prime}\cup F)}}|\Lambda^{\prime}|!\mathbf{1}_{\{\tau=\Lambda\cup C^{\prime},\lambda\setminus\tau=\Lambda^{\prime}\}}

Now note that
|\Lambda|=|\tau|-1
and
|\Lambda^{\prime}|=|\lambda|-|\tau|
so we can pull these terms outside of the
F,\Lambda,C^{\prime},\Lambda^{\prime}
sums. After this, we are simply counting how many ways we can choose
F,\Lambda,C^{\prime},\Lambda^{\prime}
to satisfy the constraint. There are precisely
|\tau|
different ways:
C^{\prime}
is any block of
\tau
,
F
is the union of the remaining blocks,
\Lambda
is chosen to be
\tau\setminus\{C^{\prime}\}
, and
\Lambda^{\prime}=\lambda\setminus\tau
. Thus this sum is simply

\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|-1}\sum_{\tau:\emptyset\neq\tau\subset\lambda}|\tau|(|\tau|-1)!(|\lambda|-|\tau|)!\\
\times\prod_{B\in\tau}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\lambda\setminus\tau}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}\\
=-\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|}\sum_{\tau:\emptyset\neq\tau\subset\lambda}|\tau|!(|\lambda|-|\tau|)!\\
\times\prod_{B\in\tau}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\lambda\setminus\tau}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

Combining the two cases

We thus find that adding the contribution from both of the cases gives

\displaystyle\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}\!(-1)^{|\lambda|}\!\sum_{\tau:\tau\subset\lambda}\!|\tau|!(|\lambda|-|\tau|)!\prod_{B\in\tau}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\!\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\lambda\setminus\tau}\!\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|}\sum_{\tau:\emptyset\neq\tau\subset\lambda}|\tau|!(|\lambda|-|\tau|)!

\displaystyle\qquad\times\prod_{B\in\tau}\frac{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\rangle_{0}}{\langle(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{V}(\sigma\widetilde{\sigma})_{U}\rangle_{0}}\prod_{B^{\prime}\in\lambda\setminus\tau}\bigg\langle\prod_{t^{\prime}\in B^{\prime}}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t^{\prime}}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

The two terms are identical apart from the second one having the constraint
\tau\neq\emptyset
. Thus all the other terms in the
\tau
sums cancel and the only thing that is left is the
\tau=\emptyset
term yielding

\displaystyle\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|}|\lambda|!\prod_{B\in\lambda}\bigg\langle\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\bigg\rangle_{0}.

This means that for our correlation function, we have

\displaystyle\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W}\rangle_{m\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{m}{\pi}\right)^{r}\frac{1}{r!}\int_{\Omega^{r}}d^{2r}x\prod_{j=1}^{r}\alpha(x_{j})\sum_{I\subset W}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}K\subset[r]:\\
|K|=|I|\end{subarray}}\sum_{\nu:I\leftrightarrow K}\prod_{i\in I}\left(-\langle\varepsilon_{w_{i}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{w_{i}};\varepsilon_{x_{\nu(i)}}+\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{x_{\nu(i)}}\rangle_{0}^{\mathsf{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{C\subset[r]\setminus K}\bigg\langle\sigma_{V}\widetilde{\sigma}_{V}\mu_{U}\widetilde{\mu}_{U}\Psi_{Z}\widetilde{\Psi}_{Z}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{W\setminus I}\prod_{s\in C}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{s}}\bigg\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{\lambda\in\Pi_{[r]\setminus(K\cup C)}}(-1)^{|\lambda|}|\lambda|!\prod_{B\in\lambda}\bigg\langle\prod_{t\in B}(\varepsilon+\widetilde{\varepsilon})_{x_{t}}\bigg\rangle_{0},

using the moment to cumulant map (
1.4.3
) again yields the desired expansion.
∎
